# Every Q3 code

Generated by `tools/build_q_codes.py` from `docs/leica-q3-codes.json`. Do not edit by hand; edit the JSON and run the script.

Every code a Leica Q3 on firmware 4.1 writes to `settings.lcs`, in the order the camera writes them, with what each is thought to be. Background, sources and how to help: [Leica Q cameras](leica-q.md).

**How sure.** Known: two independent sources agree, or the camera's own screen or the photographer's own words match their own file on a setting changed from factory (95 codes). Likely: one strong clue, such as the manual's option list matching the values (218). Guess: a clue that fits but proves nothing, such as a code's neighbors (169). Unknown: no clue (105). None of it has been tested by importing an edited file on a Q3.

**Columns.** Kind: number or text, per profile (seven values: the live settings, then profiles 1 to 6) or camera-wide (one value). Since: the first firmware whose files hold the code (1-2, 3.0, 3.1 or 4). Factory: the value in the four profiles Ken Rockwell never saved in his Q3 file (firmware 4.1.0). Q3 43: the same photographer's Q3 43 file holds a different value, because of the lens or the photographer's own choice.

**Words in the evidence.** Hudson, Hudson's profiles: the Q3 and Q3 43 files Hudson Henry shares (firmware 4.1.1), with six named profiles (Standard, B&W, LS / Pano, People, Animals, Bracketing). Ken Rockwell's file: his Q3 file (firmware 4.1.0) with two saved profiles and four never saved, read with his guide, his sample photos and his video. Froschmayer: Florian Froschmayer's Q3 and Q3 43 files (firmware 3.1) and button guides, and his SL3 and SL3-S files. Forum exports: Q3 files Leica forum members posted in 2024 (firmware 1 to 2). Old file: a Q3 file on firmware 3.0, from the Leica Dial & Dot repository; its unsaved profiles hold factory values. Screens: camera menus shown in Hudson Henry's, Florian Froschmayer's and Ken Rockwell's videos, read against their own files. Photo metadata: the Leica maker note in photos straight from a Q3. SL3 files: Nick Rains' and Jono Slack's, which their authors describe in words. USB list: what a Q3 43 on firmware 4.1.1 reports over USB, as published by libgphoto2. M code book, M firmware: the M11 family's own settings tables, for codes the two lines share. SL3 editor, Dial & Dot: two hobby editors, given little weight. Manual: the Leica Q3 instructions.

| `D054` | Item list, photo: item 1 (Favorites or a function list; open) | Favorites? (open; the FN button custom list is D1FA) | a job number, see [button jobs](#button-jobs); 0 empty slot (list ends) | likely | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 (Ken Rockwell's never-saved profiles 3 to 6 hold 0, 0, 6, 10) | differs | Ordered, zero-terminated list of function numbers (same numbering as the buttons). Hudson fills items 1-11 (4,6,7,5,43,44,45,46,92,9,10), Q3 43 items 1-5 (43,16,30,32,8); all 0 in the old file and on the SL3. Item 11 lands in D070 while D05E stays 0, so D05E-D06C are not list slots. First read as the per-mode On/Off list behind 'Customize Control > FN Buttons' (manual p. 54): Hudson's entries are all button-assignable and include Interval and Bracketing, which the Control Center cannot hold. Favorites is the alternative reading. The FN button custom lists are D1FA and D1FB, so this list is probably something else, most likely Favorites: items can be setting codes as well as job numbers (a 2024 forum export holds 53318 = 0xD046, image stabilization, in this list), as in the M's Favorites (D1A9), whose items are codes of other settings or menu numbers. |

| `5013` | Drive Mode | Drive Mode | 0 Single; 5 Continuous 2 fps, 14 bit, AF (order guess); 6 Continuous 4 fps, 14 bit, AF (order guess); 14 Continuous 5 fps, 12 bit, AF (order guess); 11 Continuous 7 fps, 14 bit (order guess); 7 Continuous 9 fps, 12 bit (order guess); 9 Continuous 15 fps, 12 bit (order guess); 2 Interval Shooting; 8 Exposure Bracketing | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 | differs | PTP standard + M code book + USB list (9 values = manual's 9 options); 8 only in the Bracketing profile. Continuous speeds mapped by USB list order. |
| `500A` | Focus Mode (photo) | Focusing > Focus Mode | 0 AFs; 1 AFc; 5 Intelligent AF | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 5 |  | PTP standard + USB (1 = AFc); AFc in People/Animals profiles; 5 = iAF because the video copy D0E0 holds 5 and the manual's video factory is iAF. Old file's unsaved slots hold 5 although this manual says photo factory AFs. Ken Rockwell's guide says AF-S; his saved slots hold 0, the never-saved ones 5 (Intelligent AF). |
| `D137` | AF Mode (photo) | Focusing > AF Mode | 12 Field / Spot (factory since firmware 3.1); 13 Zone / Multi-Field; 6 Tracking? (by elimination: the USB list is 6, 12, 13); 5 Field (numbering before firmware 3.1; the factory value then); 4 Spot (before firmware 3.1; SL3 launch firmware); 7 Eye / Face / Body detection (before firmware 3.1); 11 Animal detection (before firmware 3.1); 255 not set (LS / Pano profile) | likely | number per profile | 1-2 | 12 | differs | SL3 label AFMethod + USB list of 3 values = manual's 3 AF modes; 13 in People/Animals. Value mapping by list order only. Old firmware used 5. Firmware 4's quick menu offers Field / Spot, Zone / Multi-Field and Tracking. Hudson Henry's Standard profile uses Field (file 12) and his People and Animals profiles the middle Zone (13); Froschmayer always uses Field and holds 12 in his 3.1 file and 5 in his older file. This reverses the earlier list-order guess (12 = Zone / Multi-Field). Older numbering, from Jono Slack's SL3 article against his file: Day 'Spot Focus' 4, Animal 11, Mono and Portrait 'Eye / Face / Body Detection' 7, iAuto 'Field Focusing' 5. In 3.1 detection moved to its own setting (D0BF) and the factory value changed from 5 to 12. Two forum members' profiles named 'portrait' and 'face&eyes' (2024) hold 7, as do several of their other profiles. |
| `D138` | AF field size (photo) | Focusing > AF Mode > field size (thumbwheel) | 3 Hudson Henry's LS / Pano (a spot-sized field); 4 factory; Hudson's Standard ('medium field size'); 5 Hudson's People and Animals ('about that middle zone'); 6 Froschmayer, every profile | likely | number per profile | 3.0 | 4 | differs | Follows D137; 3 in LS / Pano, 4 Standard, 5 People/Animals (Q3 43: 5, LS 6). Manual: frame size adjustable in 3 steps. Dial & Dot calls it AF tracking sensitivity. Appears with D349, D34A and D34B on two bodies in the release whose notes say configured AF field sizes are now kept in the user profile: Q3 3.0.0 (December 2024) and SL3 2.0.1. Hudson Henry's firmware 4.0 video: Standard uses a medium field, People and Animals 'about that middle zone'; firmware 4 resizes the field or zone with the thumbwheel. The scale is not mapped. |
| `500B` | Exposure Metering (photo) | Exposure Metering | 0 Spot; 1 Center-Weighted; 32769 Highlight-Weighted; 2 Multi-Field | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 2 |  | PTP + M code book + USB list in manual order; Highlight-Weighted in LS / Pano; factory Multi-Field = 2. The public USB label disagrees: Public: 2 = Center Weighted, 1 unlabeled. Draft: 1 = Center-Weighted, 2 = Multi-Field. The M firmware menu labels (1 Center-weighted, 2 Multi-field) and the factory value back the draft. The public label equals the generic PTP name for 2 ("Center Weighted Average") and the flip never produced value 1, so it looks carried over rather than observed. Draft probably right; one metering flip on a Q3 settles it. Jono Slack's SL3 article names each profile's metering: his four 'Spot Metering' profiles hold 0 and his 'Multi Point Metering' profile (iAuto) holds 2. This backs 2 = Multi-Field against the libgphoto2 label; 1 is in no file. |
| `5010` | Exposure Compensation | Exposure Compensation | -3000..3000 thousandths of a stop in 1/3 steps | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 | differs | PTP + M code book + USB list (-3000..3000); manual ±3 EV in 1/3 steps. |

| `D007` | EV Steps (bracketing) | Drive Mode > Exposure Bracketing > EV Steps | 333..3000 1/3 to 3 EV in thousandths | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 1000 |  | M code book (333..3000) + manual (up to 3 EV); Q3 file 3000 = 3 EV, old file 1000. |
| `D008` | Automatic (bracketing) | Drive Mode > Exposure Bracketing > Automatic | 1 On (factory) | likely | number per profile | 1-2 | 1 |  | Comes after Number of Frames/EV Steps; 1 everywhere; manual factory for Automatic is On. M leaves it unnamed; Dial & Dot calls it long-exposure noise reduction. Three screens show Automatic On over 1 (Hudson Henry twice, Froschmayer), and Hudson explains the setting. Off was never shown. |
| `D042` |  |  |  | unknown | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 |  | No reliable name. 0 in every slot; same in old file. |
| `D000` | Number of Frames (interval) | Drive Mode > Interval Shooting > Number of Frames | 1..9999 frames | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 300 |  | M code book Interval Frames + manual; old file 300 = M default. Photo metadata (maker-note tag 0x0425) records 300 frames, as in Ken Rockwell's file. |
| `D001` | Interval | Drive Mode > Interval Shooting > Interval | 1..89999 seconds | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 2 |  | M code book Interval Time (s) + manual; value 2 = M default. Photo metadata (maker-note tag 0x0425) records the interval: 2 s on 10 June and 10 s from 11 June in Ken Rockwell's photos; his file holds 10 in his saved slots and 2 in the never-saved ones. |
| `D06E` | Countdown | Drive Mode > Interval Shooting > Countdown | 0..89999 seconds | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 |  | M code book Interval Countdown + manual; value 0 = M default. |
| `D003` | Shutter Speed Limit (Flash) | ISO Settings > Auto ISO Settings > Shutter Speed Limit (Flash) | 10 Auto (factory) | likely | number per profile | 1-2 | 10 |  | M code book; manual has the item (factory Auto). Q3 and old file hold 10, which is not in the M's value list, so the Q3 encoding differs. Both of Hudson Henry's Q3 videos show Shutter Speed Limit (Flash) Auto; his file holds 10, the factory value. |
| `D041` | Flash Exp. Compensation | Flash Settings > Flash Exp. Compensation | -3000..3000 thousandths of a stop; -1333 -1 1/3 EV (Hudson Henry's Q2) | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 |  | M code book; manual factory 0 EV = file 0. Hudson Henry's Q2 screen shows Flash Exp. Compensation -1 1/3 EV; his Q2 file holds -1333 (factory 0). Same code on the Q3. |
| `D009` | Flash Sync | Flash Settings > Flash Sync | 0 Start?; 1 End? | likely | number per profile | 1-2 | 1 |  | M code book; manual options Start/End, factory End; Q3 and old file 1. |
| `D07F` | Flash Mode? | Flash Settings > Flash Mode |  | guess | number per profile | 1-2 | 2 |  | Is in the flash run (D003, D041, D009) before ISO; value 2 everywhere incl. old file. Manual: Automatic/Manual/Long-term, factory Automatic. Profile names point both ways, so this stays a guess: the SL2 'STUDIO' and SL2-S 'Flash' profiles are the only SL2 profiles with 3, but on the Q3 Froschmayer holds 3 in every profile and a 2024 forum export holds 3 in non-flash profiles too. Ken Rockwell says he set flash always on and holds 2, the factory value. The 4.0.0 notes changed the factory Flash Mode from Auto to On. |

Photo metadata (maker-note tag 0x0428 byte 0) reads 0 in Ken Rockwell's JPG-only photos (file 2) and 1 in DNG + JPG review photos; the tag has its own numbering. |
| `D04F` | Aspect Ratio | Aspect Ratio | 0 3:2 (factory); 2 4:3 | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 |  | SL3 label AspectRatio + manual factory 3:2 = file 0. Other two (1:1, 16:9) unmapped. Photo metadata (maker-note tag 0x042A) reads 2 in all 12 of Ken Rockwell's 4:3 photos and 0 in his 3:2 photo and in 64 Q3 Monochrom photos (3:2). His file holds 2 in the live slot and in the profile he named '4*3 full', 0 in profile 1. |

Hudson Henry's switch is off and his file holds 0 here while D01A stays at the factory -3; Froschmayer turned review off and holds 0 in both files; Ken Rockwell uses a 5 s review (D01A = 5) and holds 1. The Q2 has no D03F and stores Off as D01A = 0. |

| `D08A` | Standard Film Style (photo): Contrast, firmware 1 to 3 | Film Style > Standard > Contrast (firmware 1 to 3) | -2..2 level; 0 = factory | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 |  | M firmware names it Film Mode 1 Contrast; the SL3 configurator also calls it Contrast for Standard. Manual: Core Looks have Contrast -2..+2. 0 in every slot. Not where firmware 4 keeps Look adjustments: Ken Rockwell set Vivid with +2 Saturation and +2 Sharpening on firmware 4.1.0 (his guide and photos), and these codes are 0 in every slot of his file; the change is in DA03. Two 2024 forum exports (firmware 1 to 2) vary D08A, D093, D1AE and D0B1, so the codes were live before the DA Looks records (first seen in 3.1 files). |
| `D08B` | Standard Film Style (photo): Sharpness, firmware 1 to 3 | Film Style > Standard > Sharpness (firmware 1 to 3) | -2..2 level; 0 = factory | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 |  | M firmware names it Film Mode 1 Sharpness (values -2..2). The SL3 configurator says Highlight/Shadow here, but the count settles it: Standard, Vivid and Natural have 3 old codes each, the two monochrome Looks 2 each (3+3+3+2+2 = 13), which only works if the third old parameter is Saturation, the one a monochrome Look lacks. All 0. Not where firmware 4 keeps Look adjustments: Ken Rockwell set Vivid with +2 Saturation and +2 Sharpening on firmware 4.1.0 (his guide and photos), and these codes are 0 in every slot of his file; the change is in DA03. Two 2024 forum exports (firmware 1 to 2) vary D08A, D093, D1AE and D0B1, so the codes were live before the DA Looks records (first seen in 3.1 files). Two 2024 forum exports (firmware 1 to 2) vary D08A, D093, D1AE and D0B1, so the codes were live before the DA Looks records (first seen in 3.1 files). Jono Slack's SL3 'Mono' profile ('high contrast Monochrome') holds +2 here, 0 elsewhere. A 2024 forum export's black-and-white profile ('Zwart-wit') holds -1 here. |
| `D094` | Monochrome Film Style (photo): Sharpness, firmware 1 to 3 | Film Style > Monochrome > Sharpness (firmware 1 to 3) | -2..2 level; 0 = factory | likely | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 |  | M firmware names it Film Mode 4 Sharpness (values -2..2). The SL3 configurator says Highlight/Shadow here, but the count settles it: Standard, Vivid and Natural have 3 old codes each, the two monochrome Looks 2 each (3+3+3+2+2 = 13), which only works if the third old parameter is Saturation, the one a monochrome Look lacks. All 0. Hudson Henry's USB Mode popup opens on Mass Storage and he says he uses it; his files hold 0. Froschmayer's screen shows Select on Connection and his files hold 2, the factory value (Ken Rockwell's never-saved profiles; the 3.0.0 release notes made Select on Connection the factory setting). Nick Rains' SL3 PDF: 'USB Mode: Select on Connection', file 2. The M firmware labels 1 PTP and 3 Apple MFi on this code. The Q3's open-source package includes the iPhone-over-USB tools (libimobiledevice, usbmuxd), which fits an Apple option. |

| `D13E` | Film Style (photo), pre-firmware-4 selector |  | 0 Standard (factory; also 0 in every firmware 3.1 and 4 file, black-and-white profiles included); 1 Vivid (guess); 2 Natural (guess); 3 Monochrome (likely: black-and-white profiles hold 3 or 4; order from the M list); 4 Monochrome High Contrast (likely, as 3); USB the USB list gives 1 to 5 in this order, one higher than the file values | likely | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 |  | M firmware name Film Mode with values 1-5 in this order; Q3 43 and SL3 USB both list [1..5]; the 2023 Wi-Fi menu list has colormode = standard, vivid, natural, bw, l_bw. 0 in every Hudson slot and in the old file, so firmware 4 selects with D69C; the code probably survives for USB/remote use. File values look zero-based, one lower than the USB list: every factory slot holds 0 (Standard), and black-and-white profiles hold 3 or 4 in four owners' files on two lines: two forum members' black-and-white profiles ('B&W' 4, 'Zwart-wit' 3), Jono Slack's SL3 'Mono' 3, the SL3 Landscape set's 'Leica Mono' 4. Under a 1-to-5 reading 3 would be Natural. Photo metadata (maker-note tag 0x0411) uses 1 to 5 in this order: 1 with Standard and 5 with Monochrome High Contrast (Q3 43, firmware 2.0.4). |

| `D1AE` | Standard Film Style (photo): Highlight, firmware 1 to 3 (pair order is a guess) | Film Style > Standard > Highlight (firmware 1 to 3) | -2..2 level; 0 = factory | likely | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 |  | Ten codes = five Looks x the two parameters the M lacks (manual: Contrast / Highlight / Shadow / Sharpness / Saturation; M firmware has only Contrast/Sharpness/Saturation). Pairs follow Look order: the SL3 configurator groups them the same way (D1AE/D1AF Standard ... D1B6/D1B7 Monochrome HC). Which of the pair is Highlight and which Shadow follows the manual's menu order only. Present (0) in the old file too. All 0. Not where firmware 4 keeps Look adjustments: Ken Rockwell set Vivid with +2 Saturation and +2 Sharpening on firmware 4.1.0 (his guide and photos), and these codes are 0 in every slot of his file; the change is in DA03. | `D1ED` | Thumbwheel function in P (photo) | Customize Control > Thumbwheel | 0 Program Shift (factory); 1 Exposure Compensation; 255 not set in this profile | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 | differs | SL3 editor 'BackWheel_P_Photo'. SL3 editor table: 0 Program Shift, 1 Exposure compensation, 2 Auto ISO, 3 ISO, 4 Shutter Speed, 5 Aperture. In the old file the factory is 0 in all four photo modes and the manual says the factory thumbwheel setting is Auto. Hudson sets A = 1 and M = 3 (exposure compensation in A, shutter speed in M); Q3 43 uses 1 in M (3 in LS / Pano). Hudson Henry's screen shows Program AE Mode = Ps over 0. Ken Rockwell's screen shows EV over 1 and his guide says he set the dial to exposure compensation; his saved profiles hold 1, his never-saved ones 0. |
| `D1EE` | Thumbwheel function in S (photo) | Customize Control > Thumbwheel | 0 factory; the screen shows Exposure Compensation (Hudson Henry, Ken Rockwell); 255 not set in this profile | likely | number per profile | 1-2 | 0 | differs | SL3 editor 'BackWheel_S_Photo'. SL3 editor table: 0 Program Shift, 1 Exposure compensation, 2 Auto ISO, 3 ISO, 4 Shutter Speed, 5 Aperture. In the old file the factory is 0 in all four photo modes and the manual says the factory thumbwheel setting is Auto. Hudson sets A = 1 and M = 3 (exposure compensation in A, shutter speed in M); Q3 43 uses 1 in M (3 in LS / Pano). Both owners' screens show EV in Shutter priority while this code holds 0, and EV in Aperture priority while D1EF holds 1. So the numbers are positions in each mode's own list, or the S and A codes are the other way round; Nick Rains' SL3 file ties D1EF to Aperture priority. Test: set S to ISO and export. |
| `D1EF` | Thumbwheel function in A (photo) | Customize Control > Thumbwheel | 0 factory before firmware 3.1; 1 Exposure Compensation (factory since firmware 3.1); 255 not set in this profile | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 1 | differs | SL3 editor 'BackWheel_A_Photo'. SL3 editor table: 0 Program Shift, 1 Exposure compensation, 2 Auto ISO, 3 ISO, 4 Shutter Speed, 5 Aperture. In the old file the factory is 0 in all four photo modes and the manual says the factory thumbwheel setting is Auto. Hudson sets A = 1 and M = 3 (exposure compensation in A, shutter speed in M); Q3 43 uses 1 in M (3 in LS / Pano). Nick Rains' SL3 menu PDF: 'Aperture Priority: Thumb - Exp Comp'; his SL3 file holds 1 here in the slots he set up and 0 in the untouched ones. On the Q3, Hudson Henry's and Ken Rockwell's screens show EV in Aperture priority with 1 here. The factory value changed from 0 to 1 by firmware 3.1. |
| `D1F1` | Thumbwheel function in M (photo) | Customize Control > Thumbwheel | 0 factory before firmware 3.1; 1 Exposure Compensation (factory since firmware 3.1); 2 ISO; 3 Shutter Speed (1/3 steps around the dial setting); 255 not set in this profile | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 1 | differs | SL3 editor 'BackWheel_M_Photo'. SL3 editor table: 0 Program Shift, 1 Exposure compensation, 2 Auto ISO, 3 ISO, 4 Shutter Speed, 5 Aperture. In the old file the factory is 0 in all four photo modes and the manual says the factory thumbwheel setting is Auto. Hudson sets A = 1 and M = 3 (exposure compensation in A, shutter speed in M); Q3 43 uses 1 in M (3 in LS / Pano). Values read from screens against the same people's files: Hudson Henry set the thumbwheel in Manual mode to shutter speed in April 2026 (ATS 404), and his December 2025 screen shows M = S when he switches it; his file holds 3 in every profile. Ken Rockwell's screen shows M = ISO with his profile on 2; his other saved profile holds 3. These are per-mode values. The SL3 editor's single list (0 Program Shift, 1 Exposure Compensation, 2 Auto ISO, 3 ISO, 4 Shutter Speed, 5 Aperture), used by the earlier entry and by the text lane, does not hold for the Q3 in M. The factory value changed from 0 to 1 by firmware 3.1 (Froschmayer's 3.1 file and Ken Rockwell's never-saved profiles hold 1; every older file 0). Froschmayer's Q3 PDF labels the thumbwheel ISO while his file holds the factory 1 here; the label is a drawing error (the button on the same wheel is labeled User Profiles), not evidence. |

Hudson sets A = 1 and M = 3 (exposure compensation in A, shutter speed in M); Q3 43 uses 1 in M (3 in LS / Pano). | `D1F0` | unknown (between the A and M thumbwheel codes) |  | 3 ISO? (guess) | unknown | number per profile | 1-2 | 3 |  | 3 in every file, including the SL3 default. Possibly a fifth exposure-mode slot. |
| `D1FA` | FN button custom list, photo | Customize Control > FN Buttons > custom list (the menu a long press opens) | format per profile slot: 36, a count N, then N job numbers; the seven slots follow one another; [36, 1, 95] factory record (a list of one) | likely | number list per profile | 3.1 | [36, 1, 95] |  | 21 numbers = 7 profiles x 3 in Hudson's files. Always 36, 1, 95 in every slot: both Hudson files, and the SL3 default file. The old Q3 firmware had eight such codes (D1FD-D200, D204-D207) with the same triple; 3.1 and 4 keep two. Shape fits the M Favorites header (first value, count, items) with count 1 and item 95, and the files below hold real lists in this shape. All 98 list-shaped values in 30 Q3 and SL files read as [36, N, then N job numbers] per slot, with nothing left over; Ken Rockwell's file holds lists of 16 and 4 items. The manual's 'Creating the custom list' (Customize Control > FN Button 1 / FN Button 2 / Center Button / Thumbwheel Button > On or Off for each menu item, separately for photo and video) describes eight lists, which is what the Q3 kept up to 3.0 (D1FD to D200 photo, D204 to D207 video). From 3.1 it keeps one per mode; a firmware 4 forum thread says one list now serves all function buttons and the thumbwheel. |

Always 36, 1, 95 in every slot: both Hudson files, and the SL3 default file. The old Q3 firmware had eight such codes (D1FD-D200, D204-D207) with the same triple; 3.1 and 4 keep two. Shape fits the M Favorites header (first value, count, items) with count 1 and item 95, and D1FA's files hold real lists in this shape. Video twin of D1FA (see there). |

The old file has 0 (setting may not have existed). Ken Rockwell's never-saved profiles hold 0, so 2 is not the 4.1.0 factory value. Hudson Henry switched Wind Noise Reduction off on screen in 2023 (one item then); this is the only wind code off factory in his file. Either this is the setting the old item moved into, or the internal / external split is the other way round. |
| `D69C` | Leica Looks (photo): selected Look ID | Leica Looks | 15 Standard; 12 Vivid (the camera's own photo metadata names 12 'Vivid'); 13 Natural; 16 Monochrome; 11 Monochrome High Contrast; 21 a downloaded Look, which one unknown (Hudson Henry's Q3, Looks list slot 6); 1 Leica Contemporary (Wi-Fi capture, firmware 3.0.0); 2 Leica Classic (Wi-Fi capture); 3-5 probably Blue, Selenium and Sepia, order unknown (guess); 6 Leica Eternal (Wi-Fi capture); and 4 more | known | number per profile | 1-2 | 15 | differs | Identity known (draft: USB 'Leica Looks', 0x2000000F = STD). New here: the value set. The Looks list DA01.. holds 15, 12, 13, 16, 11 in slots 1-5 on both bodies; the manual lists the five pre-installed Looks as Standard, Vivid, Natural, Monochrome, Monochrome High Contrast. Slot order = manual order is confirmed at slot 1 (15 = STD on USB) and is consistent at slot 4 (16 in the B&W profile). Names for 13 and 11 rest on order only; 12 is confirmed below. The camera's own photo metadata: maker-note tag 0x041C holds the Look ID in this numbering next to tag 0x0412 with the Look's name. Ken Rockwell's 13 photos (firmware 4.1.0) read 12 and 'Vivid'; his file holds 12 in his saved slots and his guide says he set Vivid. Look IDs 1, 2, 6 and 8 come from a Wi-Fi capture of a Q3 on firmware 3.0.0 (leica_q3_hack notes); the known IDs rise with release date. Q3 Monochrom photos (firmware 4.0.0) give 17 Monochrome, 18 Selenium, 19 Blue, 20 Sepia. Files before 3.1 hold 7 in almost every slot (one owner's color, tripod and flash profiles hold 2). 7, one past the old menu's six Looks slots, reads as no Look selected. A converter must not copy 7 across, since 7 is a Look ID in the new numbering. The factory value changed from 7 to 15 by firmware 3.1. |

| `D296` | Info profile 1: clipping or zebra threshold | Capture Assistants > Clipping / Zebra (open) | 99 every Q3 file (253 of 255 is 99 %); 253 SL3 files of Nick Rains and Jono Slack; 233 the SL3 Landscape set | guess | number per profile | 3.1 | 99 |  | Firmware-4 block D27E-D316 is video Capture Assistants: four info profiles (manual p. 160-161: 'configured and saved individually for Photo and Video mode'), fields in the manual's table order (Info Bars, Grids, Zebra, Focus Peaking + color + sensitivity, Level Gauge, Histogram, Framelines), each field listed for profiles 1-4. Pairs with the zebra on/off codes; 99 in all four. The manual shows no zebra level. Nick Rains' SL3 PDF: 'Clip ON - 253' for his info profiles; his and Jono Slack's SL3 files hold 253 in D296, D298, D29A and D29C. On the SL3 these four are a clipping limit on a 0 to 255 scale, one per info profile, set in photo mode. Whether the Q3 uses them for photo clipping, video zebra or both is open. |

| `DA01` | Leica Looks list, Core Look slot 1 (photo) | Leica Looks | chars 0-7 Look ID, u32 big-endian hex (0000000F = 15 = Standard); 0 = empty slot; chars 8-9 Intensity in hex: 64 = 100 % (likely); chars 10-14 the Look's Customize levels in the manual's order: Contrast, Highlight, Shadow, Sharpness, Saturation; 1 at factory; Ken Rockwell's +2 reads 2, so the scale is open; chars 15-31 zero in every slot of both files; Q3 value 0000000F641111100000000000000000; Q3 43 value 0000000F641111100000000000000000 | likely | text per profile | 3.1 | 0000000F641111100000000000000000 |  | 16-byte record as 32 hex digits, same in all 7 profile slots of Hudson's files. First word = Look ID in the same numbering as D69C/D69D. 11 slots = 5 pre-installed Core Looks + 6 slots for downloaded Looks (the 2023 Wi-Fi menu list had exactly six 'Leica Look 1-6' slots). Q3 holds 15, 12, 13, 16, 11, 21; Q3 43 holds 15, 12, 13, 16, 11 and an empty slot 6. Odd and even codes are identical twins; odd = photo, even = video (see below). Byte 4 = 100 in every slot including empty ones, so it is a default, probably Intensity (manual: 'Intensity can be set as a percentage'). The five 1-nibbles never vary in Hudson's files. Records differ between profile slots: Ken Rockwell set Vivid with +2 Saturation and +2 Sharpening (guide, firmware 4.1.0), and only DA03 (photo, Vivid) changes, only in his two saved profiles and the live slot, from 0000000C6411111... to 0000000C6411122...; DA04 (video, Vivid) stays at the default. His photos show both changes (maker-note tag 0x0406). So odd codes are photo and even codes video, and digits 13 and 14 are Sharpness and Saturation in the manual's order (which is which cannot be told from this file). Look IDs: see D69C. The 3.1 files already hold an intensity per Look, about seven months before firmware 4.0.0 announced the Intensity control. |

His photos show both changes (maker-note tag 0x0406). | `DA0F` | Leica Looks list, downloaded Look slot 3 (photo) | Leica Looks | chars 0-7 Look ID, u32 big-endian hex (0000000F = 15 = Standard); 0 = empty slot; chars 8-9 Intensity in hex: 64 = 100 % (likely); chars 10-14 the Look's Customize levels in the manual's order: Contrast, Highlight, Shadow, Sharpness, Saturation; 1 at factory; Ken Rockwell's +2 reads 2, so the scale is open; chars 15-31 zero in every slot of both files; Q3 value 00000000641111100000000000000000; Q3 43 value 00000000641111100000000000000000 | guess | text per profile | 3.1 | 00000000641111100000000000000000 |  | 16-byte record as 32 hex digits, same in all 7 profile slots of Hudson's files. First word = Look ID in the same numbering as D69C/D69D. 11 slots = 5 pre-installed Core Looks + 6 slots for downloaded Looks (the 2023 Wi-Fi menu list had exactly six 'Leica Look 1-6' slots). His photos show both changes (maker-note tag 0x0406). So odd codes are photo and even codes video, and digits 13 and 14 are Sharpness and Saturation in the manual's order (which is which cannot be told from this file). Look IDs: see D69C. Detection became its own setting in 3.1 (3.1.1 added the AF Settings section); before, it was a value of AF Mode. |

For: firmware 4.0.0 added favorite digital zoom levels, kept separately for photo and video (release notes; manual: Customize Control > Digital Zoom Steps, levels 28, 35, 50, 75, 90 mm); the run is new in firmware 4 and splits 4 + 4; Froschmayer's SL3 4.2.0 file has the firmware-4 Control Center codes but not this run, so it is Q-only. Against: Hudson Henry's firmware 4.0 video (December 2025, 00:24:37) shows all four photo steps on, while his file and the factory hold 1, 1, 0, 1. Which level each code is, and how the Q3 43's lengths fit, is open. |

## Button jobs

One numbering covers the buttons, the FN button custom lists (`D1FA`, `D1FB`), the item lists (`D054` and `D10A` runs) and the Control Center tiles.

| Number | Job | How sure | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| 3 | Magnification | known | SL3 table 'Magnification'; M list 'Focus Aid' (the M's magnifier). Factory video Center button in the old Q3 file. Froschmayer's SL3 PDF labels the front upper button Magnification; his SL3 4.2.0 file holds 3 there. |
| 4 | Drive Mode | known | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; first item of Hudson's photo list; is in the Control Center slot the manual shows as Drive Mode. Froschmayer's SL3 PDF: Drive Mode on the top button, file 4. |
| 5 | Self-timer | known | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; follows Bracketing in Hudson's photo list, as in the manual's menu order. A forum member's 'TIME LASPE' profile (2024) puts 5 on the thumbwheel button. |
| 6 | Interval Shooting | known | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; follows Drive Mode in Hudson's photo list. |
| 7 | Exposure Bracketing | known | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; follows Interval Shooting in Hudson's photo list. |
| 8 | Capture Assistants | known | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; last item of both Q3 43 lists. The SL3 editor's table and the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files, in menu order) agree on it. |
| 9 | Exposure Metering | known | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; follows the focus items in both of Hudson's lists. |
| 10 | Exposure Compensation | known | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; Hudson's video thumbwheel button. |
| 11 | ISO (the Q3 menu calls it ISO Settings) | known | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; Q3 factory thumbwheel button is 11 in the old file and 'ISO Settings' in the manual (p. 40 and p. 228). The camera's own label 'ISO' in photo metadata (maker-note tag 0x042D) for the factory thumbwheel button and for Ken Rockwell's FN 2. Nick Rains' SL3 PDF changes a button from WB to ISO and his file goes from 15 to 11; Froschmayer's SL3 PDF: ISO, file 11. |
| 12 | Auto ISO Settings | known | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; Q3 43 FN 2 switches between 11 and 12 per profile. |
| 13 | Maximum ISO | guess | M list only. |
| 14 | Shutter Speed Limit | guess | M list only. |
| 15 | White Balance | known | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; between Auto ISO and Gray Card in Hudson's video list. Nick Rains' SL3 PDF: top-right button 'WB', file 15 in his untouched slots; Froschmayer's SL3 PDF: White Balance on the video top button, file 15. |
| 16 | Gray Card | likely | SL3 table (the M uses 17); directly after White Balance in Hudson's video list. |
| 18 | Photo File Format | likely | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; not seen in a Q3 file. Seen since in the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files). |
| 19 | DNG Resolution | likely | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; not seen in a Q3 file. Seen since in the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files). |
| 21 | Digital Zoom (in review mode: Zoom) | known | M list 'Digital Zoom'; Q3 factory FN 1 is Digital Zoom (manual p. 40) and 21 in the old file, photo and video. Review: thumbwheel button factory 'Zoom' (manual p. 231) = 21. The camera's own label 'Digital Zoom' for FN 1 in photo metadata (maker-note tag 0x042D). Froschmayer's Q3 PDF: video FN 1 Digital Zoom, file 21. |
| 22 | Film Style (firmware 1 to 3; the firmware 4 Looks tile holds 131) | likely | M and SL3 agree on 22, but the Q3's Looks tile in the Control Center holds 131. Not seen as a Q3 button value. The Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files) place 22 between JPG resolution (94?) and iDR (56), where Film Style is in the menu; the M and SL3 tables also give 22 = Film Style. |
| 23 | Shutter Type | likely | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; not seen in a Q3 file. Seen since in the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files). |
| 24 | Flash Settings | likely | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; not seen in a Q3 file. Seen since in the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files). |
| 25 | Flash Exp. Compensation | likely | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; not seen in a Q3 file. |
| 26 | User Profile | known | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; is in the video Control Center slot the manual shows as User Profile. Froschmayer's Q3 PDF labels the thumbwheel button User Profiles (photo and video); his file holds 26 in both. |
| 27 | EVF <> LCD | known | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; on the Q3 only seen in unused slots D1DA/D1DB and in D36C. Froschmayer's SL3 PDF labels the top-left button EVF <> LCD in video; his SL3 4.2.0 file holds 27 there. |
| 28 | Format Storage | likely | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; not seen in a Q3 file. Seen since in the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files). |
| 29 | Leica FOTOS | likely | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; not seen in a Q3 file. Seen since in the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files). |
| 30 | Delete Single (review mode) | likely | Manual p. 127 and p. 231 both give FN 1 in review = Delete Single; D190 (FN 1 review on the M) is 30 in every Q3 file. Also appears in both Q3 43 custom lists, which does not fit a review function: open. |
| 32 | Delete Unrated? (M: 'Delete All without star') | guess | M review list has 32 = Delete All without star; on the Q3 only seen in the Q3 43 custom lists, where a delete function is odd: open. |
| 36 | Photo - Video | known | SL3 table; Q3 factory FN 2 (manual p. 40 and p. 226) and 36 in the old file, photo and video. The camera's own label 'Photo - Video' for the factory FN 2 in photo metadata. Froschmayer's Q3 PDF (FN 2, file 36) and SL3 PDF (front lower button, file 36) agree. |
| 37 | Toggle Info Levels | known | SL3 table; Q3 factory Center button (manual p. 40, p. 226, p. 231) and 37 in the old file, photo and review. The camera's own label 'Toggle Info Levels' for the Center button in photo metadata. Froschmayer's Q3 PDF (Center, file 37) and SL3 PDF (rear FN, file 37) agree. |
| 38 | Rate / Unrate (review mode) | guess | Manual p. 231: FN 2 in review = Rate / Unrate; D192 = 38. Manual p. 127 says FN 2 = Magnification instead. |
| 39 | AF Detection | likely | Control Center slot 4 in both modes; manual (p. 22, p. 24, p. 45) shows AF Detection in that slot for photo and video. |
| 43 | Focusing (the submenu)? | guess | Sits just before Focus Mode (44) in Hudson's photo list; the manual lists 'Focusing' as its own assignable item right before Focus Mode. |
| 44 | Focus Mode | known | SL3 table; Control Center slot 2 in both modes, which the manual shows as Focus Mode. Froschmayer's SL3 PDF: Focus Mode on the video top button, file 44. |
| 45 | AF Mode | known | SL3 table; Control Center slot 3 in both modes, which the manual shows as AF Mode. |
| 46 | Focus Aid | likely | SL3 table 'Focus aid'; comes after AF Mode in both of Hudson's lists. Q3 menu has no item called Focus Aid; Auto Magnification is the nearest. The SL3 editor's table names 46 Focus aid and 107 Auto magnification separately; both are in the Q3's own full lists, 46 first, in menu order. |
| 50 | Optical Image Stabilization (toggle) | likely | Nick Rains' SL3 PDF: the front bottom button changed from AF Mode to 'Toggle Stabilize'; his SL3 file holds 50 there in his set-up slots and 45 in the untouched ones. The SL3 editor's table names 50 Image stabilization. |
| 56 | iDR | likely | The SL3 editor's table and the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files, in menu order) agree on it; it follows Film Style (22). |
| 69 | Long exposure noise reduction | likely | The SL3 editor's table and the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files, in menu order) agree on it ('Noise red (long exp)'). |
| 70 | Exposure Preview | likely | The SL3 editor's table and the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files, in menu order) agree on it. |
| 73 | Aspect Ratio | known | The camera's own label: the photo metadata (maker-note tag 0x042D) of Ken Rockwell's last photo reads 'Aspect ratio' for the thumbwheel button, and his file holds 73 there (factory 11); he also put 73 in Control Center tile 5 (D323). The SL3 editor's table agrees. |
| 75 | Touch AF | likely | The SL3 editor's table and the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files, in menu order) agree on it; after Focus Peaking (84), as in the manual. |
| 76 | Touch AF in EVF | likely | The SL3 editor's table and the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files, in menu order) agree on it; follows 75. |
| 78 | AF Assist Lamp | likely | The SL3 editor's table and the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files, in menu order) agree on it; after AF Mode (45), as in the manual. |
| 80 | Acoustic Signal | likely | The SL3 editor's table and the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files, in menu order) agree on it; last item in the old Q3 lists. |
| 82 | Toggle Focus Point | known | Nick Rains' SL3 PDF: the front top button changed from Magnify to Toggle Focus Point; his file holds 82 there in his set-up slots and 3 in the untouched ones. Froschmayer's Q3 PDF labels FN 1 Toggle Focus Point and his Q3 file holds 82; his SL3 PDF and file agree. The Q3 3.1.1 release notes made Toggle Focus Point assignable to a function button. The SL3 editor's table: 82. |
| 84 | Focus Peaking | likely | M firmware button list and SL3 editor table agree; not seen in a Q3 file. Seen since in the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files). |
| 85 | Clipping | likely | The SL3 editor's table and the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files, in menu order) agree on it. |
| 88 | Perspective Control | likely | Both of Hudson's bodies, every profile. Not in the M or SL3 tables. Candidates by menu: AF-L / AE-L / AF-L + AE-L, Thumbwheel, AF Frame Size, Toggle Focus Point. Hudson Henry's thumbwheel-button popup opens on Perspective Control and he says so (firmware 4.0 video, December 2025); both his files (October 2026) hold 88 there. List order and the SL3 editor's table put Perspective Control at 110; the camera's screen wins and 110 stays open. He may have changed the button between video and file. Test: set the thumbwheel button to Perspective Control and export. A 2024 forum export uses 88 on FN 2. |
| 92 | unknown focus item | unknown | After 46 and before Exposure Metering in both of Hudson's lists, so a Focusing item offered in photo and video (Touch AF, Touch AF in EVF, AF Frame Size are candidates). |
| 94 | Max. JPG Resolution? | guess | Between DNG Resolution (19) and Film Style (22) in the Q3's own lists, the place of Max. JPG-Resolution in the 2023 Wi-Fi menu; the SL3 uses 49 for its JPG resolution. A forum member puts 94 on the Center and thumbwheel buttons. |
| 104 | AF-L + AE-L | likely | 104, 105, 106 are between Digital Zoom (21) and Magnification (3) in every full Q3 list, where the firmware 4 manual's direct-access table has Exposure lock: AF-L + AE-L, AE-L, AF-L. |
| 105 | AE-L | likely | Second of the exposure-lock trio, see 104. |
| 106 | AF-L | known | Hudson's photo FN 1 on both bodies; in the Q3 43 video list directly after Gray Card (16), where Color Temperature is in the menu. SL3 table has Color temperature at 108. Hudson Henry's FN 1 popup opens on AF-L and he describes it as the autofocus lock; his FN 1 holds 106 on both bodies (factory 21). Separately, list order puts AF-L third in the exposure-lock trio 104, 105, 106. Replaces the Color Temperature guess (that is 108). |
| 107 | Magnification in video? (the SL3 editor's table: Auto Magnification) | likely | Froschmayer's SL3 PDF labels the front upper button Magnification on the video page; his SL3 4.2.0 file holds 107 there (older SL3 files hold 146). The SL3 editor's table calls 107 Auto magnification, and the Q3's lists put it after Focus Aid (46). Both readings are open. |
| 108 | Color Temperature | likely | The SL3 editor's table and the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files, in menu order) agree on it; it follows Gray Card (16) in every Q3 list, as in the manual. |
| 110 | Perspective Control on the SL3 (the editor's table); unresolved on the Q3 | guess | List order and the SL3 editor's table give Perspective Control, but Hudson Henry's screen gives Perspective Control for 88. Unresolved. |
| 111 | Microphone Gain | likely | The SL3 editor's table and the Q3's own full button lists (2024 forum files, in menu order) agree on it; only in the Q3 43 video list. |
| 115 | Video Profiles | likely | Video Control Center slot 6; manual p. 24 and p. 45 show Video Profiles there. |
| 120 | Camera Settings (menu page) | likely | Photo Control Center slot 1; manual shows the exposure mode tile there and lists Scene Mode first. Hudson Henry replaced the factory Scene Mode tile with Camera Settings in all his profiles and opens it on screen; D31B = 120. The earlier entry read 120 as Scene Mode; the factory tile is 158. |
| 124 | unknown | unknown | Q3 43 only, in D36C and D36E. |
| 131 | Leica Looks | likely | Control Center slot 8 in both modes; manual shows Leica Looks there for photo and video. |
| 158 | Scene Mode (the factory first Control Center tile, photo and video) | likely | Video Control Center slot 1; manual shows the exposure mode tile there. Factory D31B and D31C are both 158 (Ken Rockwell's never-saved profiles); Hudson Henry calls the tile he replaced 'scene mode'. |

## Firmware generations

Each firmware generation writes a fixed set of codes. Codes added at one step stay in the next.

| Since | Firmware | Codes | Files | Added | Removed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1-2 | 1.x to 2.0.5 | 344 | four Leica forum exports (March to August 2024) and two older Froschmayer Q3 43 files |  |  |
| 3.0 | 3.0.0 (December 2024) | 346 | the Leica Dial & Dot sample | `D0D2`, `D138`, `D341`, `D349`, `D34A`, `D34B` | `D01D`, `D173`, `D174`, `D175` |
| 3.1 | 3.1.1 or a later 3.x (from May 2025) | 563 | Florian Froschmayer's Q3 and Q3 43 files | 231 | `D0E3`, `D0E4`, `D1EB`, `D1EC`, `D1F2`, `D1F3`, `D1FD`, `D1FE`, `D1FF`, `D200`, `D204`, `D205`, `D206`, `D207` |
| 4 | 4.0.0 (December 2025) to 4.1.1 | 587 | Ken Rockwell's Q3 (4.1.0) and Hudson Henry's Q3 43 (4.1.1): 587 codes; Hudson Henry's Q3 (4.1.1): 586, without D17A | `D31B`, `D31C`, `D31D`, `D31E`, `D31F`, `D320`, `D321`, `D322`, `D323`, `D324`, `D325`, `D326`, `D327`, `D328`, `D329`, `D32A`, `D3DB`, `D3DC`, `D3DD`, `D3DE`, `D3DF`, `D3E0`, `D3E1`, `D3E2` |  |
